Utah Legislature HB0462 

 1&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; UTAH HOUSING AFFORDABILITY AMENDMENTS 2&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; 2022 GENERAL SESSION 3&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; STATE OF UTAH 4&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Chief Sponsor: Steve Waldrip 5&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Senate Sponsor: Jacob L. Anderegg 6&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; 7&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; LONG TITLE 8&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; General Description: 9&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;This bill modifies provisions related to affordable housing and the provision of services 10&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;related to affordable housing. 11&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Highlighted Provisions: 12&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;This bill: 13&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;defines terms; 14&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requires certain political subdivisions to adopt an implementation plan as part of the 15&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;moderate income housing element of the political subdivision's general plan; 16&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;modifies the list of strategies that a political subdivision may select, or are required 17&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to select, for implementation as part of the moderate income housing element of the 18&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;political subdivision's general plan; 19&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requires certain municipalities to develop and adopt station area plans for specified 20&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;areas surrounding public transit stations; 21&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requires certain political subdivisions to amend the political subdivision's general 22&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;plan by a specified date if the general plan does not include certain provisions 23&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;related to moderate income housing; 24&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;modifies requirements for a political subdivision's annual moderate income housing 25&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;report to the Housing and Community Development Division (division) within the 26&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Department of Workforce Services (department); 27&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;allows a political subdivision to have priority consideration for certain funds or 28&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;projects if the political subdivision demonstrates plans to implement a certain 29&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;number of moderate income housing strategies; 30&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prohibits a political subdivision from receiving certain funds if the political 31&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;subdivision fails to comply with moderate income housing reporting requirements; 32&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prohibits a political subdivision from imposing impact fees for the construction of 33&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;certain internal accessory dwelling units; 34&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requires the Point of the Mountain State Land Authority to consult with the Unified 35&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Economic Opportunity Commission in planning the development of the point of the 36&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mountain state land; 37&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;modifies requirements for a public transit district to participate in a transit-oriented 38&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;development; 39&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requires certain counties to prepare and submit a proposal to create a housing and 40&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;transit reinvestment zone by a specified date; 41&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;modifies local referenda signature requirements for local land use laws that relate to 42&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the use of land within certain transit areas; 43&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;limits the referability to voters of local land use laws that relate to the use of land 44&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;within certain transit areas; 45&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requires the division to develop a statewide database of moderate income housing 46&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;units; 47&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requires the division to develop a methodology for determining whether a political 48&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;subdivision is complying with certain moderate income housing requirements, to be 49&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;submitted to and approved by the Commission on Housing Affordability by a 50&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;certain date; 51&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;modifies the membership of the Olene Walker Housing Loan Fund Board; 52&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requires an entity that receives any money from the Olene Walker Housing Loan 53&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Fund after a certain date to provide an annual accounting to the department; 54&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;repeals certain limits on the amount of money the department may distribute from 55&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the Economic Revitalization and Investment Fund; 56&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;establishes the Rural Housing Fund, to be used by the division to provide loans for 57&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;certain moderate income housing projects in rural areas; 58&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;allows the department to use a certain amount of money from specified funds to 59&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;offset administrative costs; 60&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;allows the Private Activity Bond Review Board to transfer certain unused allotment 61&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;account funds to any other allotment account, and exempts such funds from certain 62&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;set aside requirements; 63&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;allows state entities, in addition to political subdivisions, to grant real property for 64&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;certain developments that include moderate income housing; 65&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;allows the Governor's Office of Economic Opportunity to use funds from the 66&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Industrial Assistance Account to provide financial assistance to entities offering 67&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;technical assistance to municipalities for planning; and 68&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;makes technical and conforming changes. 69&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Money Appropriated in this Bill: 70&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;This bill appropriates in fiscal year 2023: 71&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to Department of Workforce Services -- Housing and Community Development, as 72&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;a one-time appropriation: 73&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#8226;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;from the General Fund, $500,000; 74&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to Department of Workforce Services -- Housing and Community Development, as 75&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;a one-time appropriation: 76&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#8226;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;from the General Fund, $750,000; 77&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to Department of Workforce Services -- Administration, as an ongoing 78&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;appropriation: 79&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#8226;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;from the General Fund, $132,000; 80&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to Department of Workforce Services -- Housing and Community Development, as 81&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;a one-time appropriation: 82&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#8226;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;from the General Fund, $250,000; and 83&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#9656;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to Department of Workforce Services -- Housing and Community Development, as 84&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;a one-time appropriation: 85&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&#8226;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;from the General Fund, $250,000. 86&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Other Special Clauses: 87&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;This bill provides a special effective date. 88&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;This bill provides a coordination clause. 89&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; 63L-12-102 , (Renumbered from 10-8-501, as enacted by Laws of Utah 2021, Chapter 134&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;333) 135&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Utah Code Sections Affected by Coordination Clause: 136&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; 10-9a-403 , as last amended by Laws of Utah 2021, First Special Session, Chapter 3 137&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; 138&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Be it enacted by the Legislature of the state of Utah: 139&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Section 1. Section 10-9a-103 is amended to read: 140&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; 10-9a-103. Definitions. 141&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;As used in this chapter: 142&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(1) "Accessory dwelling unit" means a habitable living unit added to, created within, or 143&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;detached from a primary single-family dwelling and contained on one lot. 144&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(2) "Adversely affected party" means a person other than a land use applicant who: 145&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) owns real property adjoining the property that is the subject of a land use 146&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;application or land use decision; or 147&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) will suffer a damage different in kind than, or an injury distinct from, that of the 148&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;general community as a result of the land use decision. 149&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(3) "Affected entity" means a county, municipality, local district, special service 150&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;district under Title 17D, Chapter 1, Special Service District Act, school district, interlocal 151&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;cooperation entity established under Title 11, Chapter 13, Interlocal Cooperation Act, specified 152&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;public utility, property owner, property owners association, or the Utah Department of 153&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Transportation, if: 154&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) the entity's services or facilities are likely to require expansion or significant 155&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;modification because of an intended use of land; 156&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) the entity has filed with the municipality a copy of the entity's general or long-range 157&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;plan; or 158&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) the entity has filed with the municipality a request for notice during the same 159&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;calendar year and before the municipality provides notice to an affected entity in compliance 160&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with a requirement imposed under this chapter. 161&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(4) "Affected owner" means the owner of real property that is: 162&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) a single project; 163&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) the subject of a land use approval that sponsors of a referendum timely challenged 164&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in accordance with Subsection 20A-7-601 [ (5) ] (6) ; and 165&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) determined to be legally referable under Section 20A-7-602.8 . 166&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(5) "Appeal authority" means the person, board, commission, agency, or other body 167&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;designated by ordinance to decide an appeal of a decision of a land use application or a 168&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;variance. 169&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(6) "Billboard" means a freestanding ground sign located on industrial, commercial, or 170&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;residential property if the sign is designed or intended to direct attention to a business, product, 171&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;or service that is not sold, offered, or existing on the property where the sign is located. 172&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(7) (a) "Charter school" means: 173&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) an operating charter school; 174&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) a charter school applicant that a charter school authorizer approves in accordance 175&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with Title 53G, Chapter 5, Part 3, Charter School Authorization; or 176&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) an entity that is working on behalf of a charter school or approved charter 177&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;applicant to develop or construct a charter school building. 178&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) "Charter school" does not include a therapeutic school. 179&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(8) "Conditional use" means a land use that, because of the unique characteristics or 180&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;potential impact of the land use on the municipality, surrounding neighbors, or adjacent land 181&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uses, may not be compatible in some areas or may be compatible only if certain conditions are 182&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;required that mitigate or eliminate the detrimental impacts. 183&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(9) "Constitutional taking" means a governmental action that results in a taking of 184&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;private property so that compensation to the owner of the property is required by the: 185&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) Fifth or Fourteenth Amendment of the Constitution of the United States; or 186&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) Utah Constitution Article I, Section 22. 187&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(10) "Culinary water authority" means the department, agency, or public entity with 188&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;responsibility to review and approve the feasibility of the culinary water system and sources for 189&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the subject property. 190&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(11) "Development activity" means: 191&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) any construction or expansion of a building, structure, or use that creates additional 192&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;demand and need for public facilities; 193&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) any change in use of a building or structure that creates additional demand and need 194&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;for public facilities; or 195&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) any change in the use of land that creates additional demand and need for public 196&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;facilities. 197&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(12) (a) "Development agreement" means a written agreement or amendment to a 198&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;written agreement between a municipality and one or more parties that regulates or controls the 199&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;use or development of a specific area of land. 200&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) "Development agreement" does not include an improvement completion assurance. 201&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(13) (a) "Disability" means a physical or mental impairment that substantially limits 202&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;one or more of a person's major life activities, including a person having a record of such an 203&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;impairment or being regarded as having such an impairment. 204&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) "Disability" does not include current illegal use of, or addiction to, any federally 205&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;controlled substance, as defined in Section 102 of the Controlled Substances Act, 21 U.S.C. 206&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;802. 207&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(14) "Educational facility": 208&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) means: 209&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) a school district's building at which pupils assemble to receive instruction in a 210&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;program for any combination of grades from preschool through grade 12, including 211&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kindergarten and a program for children with disabilities; 212&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) a structure or facility: 213&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) located on the same property as a building described in Subsection (14)(a)(i); and 214&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) used in support of the use of that building; and 215&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) a building to provide office and related space to a school district's administrative 216&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;personnel; and 217&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) does not include: 218&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) land or a structure, including land or a structure for inventory storage, equipment 219&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;storage, food processing or preparing, vehicle storage or maintenance, or similar use that is: 220&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) not located on the same property as a building described in Subsection (14)(a)(i); 221&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and 222&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) used in support of the purposes of a building described in Subsection (14)(a)(i); or 223&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) a therapeutic school. 224&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(15) "Fire authority" means the department, agency, or public entity with responsibility 225&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to review and approve the feasibility of fire protection and suppression services for the subject 226&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;property. 227&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(16) "Flood plain" means land that: 228&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) is within the 100-year flood plain designated by the Federal Emergency 229&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Management Agency; or 230&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) has not been studied or designated by the Federal Emergency Management Agency 231&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;but presents a likelihood of experiencing chronic flooding or a catastrophic flood event because 232&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the land has characteristics that are similar to those of a 100-year flood plain designated by the 233&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Federal Emergency Management Agency. 234&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(17) "General plan" means a document that a municipality adopts that sets forth general 235&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;guidelines for proposed future development of the land within the municipality. 236&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(18) "Geologic hazard" means: 237&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) a surface fault rupture; 238&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) shallow groundwater; 239&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) liquefaction; 240&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(d) a landslide; 241&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(e) a debris flow; 242&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(f) unstable soil; 243&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(g) a rock fall; or 244&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(h) any other geologic condition that presents a risk: 245&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) to life; 246&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) of substantial loss of real property; or 247&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) of substantial damage to real property. 248&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(19) "Historic preservation authority" means a person, board, commission, or other 249&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;body designated by a legislative body to: 250&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) recommend land use regulations to preserve local historic districts or areas; and 251&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) administer local historic preservation land use regulations within a local historic 252&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;district or area. 253&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(20) "Hookup fee" means a fee for the installation and inspection of any pipe, line, 254&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;meter, or appurtenance that connects to a municipal water, sewer, storm water, power, or other 255&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;utility system. 256&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(21) "Identical plans" means building plans submitted to a municipality that: 257&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) are clearly marked as "identical plans"; 258&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) are substantially identical to building plans that were previously submitted to and 259&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;reviewed and approved by the municipality; and 260&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) describe a building that: 261&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) is located on land zoned the same as the land on which the building described in the 262&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;previously approved plans is located; 263&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) is subject to the same geological and meteorological conditions and the same law 264&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;as the building described in the previously approved plans; 265&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) has a floor plan identical to the building plan previously submitted to and reviewed 266&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and approved by the municipality; and 267&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iv) does not require any additional engineering or analysis. 268&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(22) "Impact fee" means a payment of money imposed under Title 11, Chapter 36a, 269&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Impact Fees Act. 270&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(23) "Improvement completion assurance" means a surety bond, letter of credit, 271&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;financial institution bond, cash, assignment of rights, lien, or other equivalent security required 272&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;by a municipality to guaranty the proper completion of landscaping or an infrastructure 273&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;improvement required as a condition precedent to: 274&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) recording a subdivision plat; or 275&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) development of a commercial, industrial, mixed use, or multifamily project. 276&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(24) "Improvement warranty" means an applicant's unconditional warranty that the 277&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;applicant's installed and accepted landscaping or infrastructure improvement: 278&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) complies with the municipality's written standards for design, materials, and 279&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;workmanship; and 280&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) will not fail in any material respect, as a result of poor workmanship or materials, 281&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;within the improvement warranty period. 282&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(25) "Improvement warranty period" means a period: 283&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) no later than one year after a municipality's acceptance of required landscaping; or 284&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) no later than one year after a municipality's acceptance of required infrastructure, 285&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;unless the municipality: 286&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) determines for good cause that a one-year period would be inadequate to protect the 287&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;public health, safety, and welfare; and 288&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) has substantial evidence, on record: 289&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) of prior poor performance by the applicant; or 290&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) that the area upon which the infrastructure will be constructed contains suspect soil 291&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and the municipality has not otherwise required the applicant to mitigate the suspect soil. 292&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(26) "Infrastructure improvement" means permanent infrastructure that is essential for 293&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the public health and safety or that: 294&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) is required for human occupation; and 295&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) an applicant must install: 296&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) in accordance with published installation and inspection specifications for public 297&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;improvements; and 298&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) whether the improvement is public or private, as a condition of: 299&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) recording a subdivision plat; 300&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) obtaining a building permit; or 301&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(C) development of a commercial, industrial, mixed use, condominium, or multifamily 302&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;project. 303&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(27) "Internal lot restriction" means a platted note, platted demarcation, or platted 304&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;designation that: 305&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) runs with the land; and 306&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) (i) creates a restriction that is enclosed within the perimeter of a lot described on 307&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the plat; or 308&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) designates a development condition that is enclosed within the perimeter of a lot 309&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;described on the plat. 310&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(28) "Land use applicant" means a property owner, or the property owner's designee, 311&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;who submits a land use application regarding the property owner's land. 312&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(29) "Land use application": 313&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) means an application that is: 314&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) required by a municipality; and 315&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) submitted by a land use applicant to obtain a land use decision; and 316&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) does not mean an application to enact, amend, or repeal a land use regulation. 317&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(30) "Land use authority" means: 318&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) a person, board, commission, agency, or body, including the local legislative body, 319&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;designated by the local legislative body to act upon a land use application; or 320&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) if the local legislative body has not designated a person, board, commission, 321&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;agency, or body, the local legislative body. 322&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(31) "Land use decision" means an administrative decision of a land use authority or 323&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;appeal authority regarding: 324&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) a land use permit; 325&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) a land use application; or 326&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) the enforcement of a land use regulation, land use permit, or development 327&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;agreement. 328&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(32) "Land use permit" means a permit issued by a land use authority. 329&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(33) "Land use regulation": 330&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) means a legislative decision enacted by ordinance, law, code, map, resolution, 331&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;specification, fee, or rule that governs the use or development of land; 332&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) includes the adoption or amendment of a zoning map or the text of the zoning code; 333&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and 334&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) does not include: 335&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) a land use decision of the legislative body acting as the land use authority, even if 336&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the decision is expressed in a resolution or ordinance; or 337&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) a temporary revision to an engineering specification that does not materially: 338&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) increase a land use applicant's cost of development compared to the existing 339&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;specification; or 340&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) impact a land use applicant's use of land. 341&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(34) "Legislative body" means the municipal council. 342&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(35) "Local district" means an entity under Title 17B, Limited Purpose Local 343&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Government Entities - Local Districts, and any other governmental or quasi-governmental 344&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;entity that is not a county, municipality, school district, or the state. 345&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(36) "Local historic district or area" means a geographically definable area that: 346&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) contains any combination of buildings, structures, sites, objects, landscape features, 347&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;archeological sites, or works of art that contribute to the historic preservation goals of a 348&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;legislative body; and 349&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) is subject to land use regulations to preserve the historic significance of the local 350&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;historic district or area. 351&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(37) "Lot" means a tract of land, regardless of any label, that is created by and shown 352&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;on a subdivision plat that has been recorded in the office of the county recorder. 353&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(38) (a) "Lot line adjustment" means a relocation of a lot line boundary between 354&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adjoining lots or between a lot and adjoining parcels in accordance with Section 10-9a-608 : 355&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) whether or not the lots are located in the same subdivision; and 356&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) with the consent of the owners of record. 357&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) "Lot line adjustment" does not mean a new boundary line that: 358&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) creates an additional lot; or 359&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) constitutes a subdivision. 360&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) "Lot line adjustment" does not include a boundary line adjustment made by the 361&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Department of Transportation. 362&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(39) "Major transit investment corridor" means public transit service that uses or 363&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;occupies: 364&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) public transit rail right-of-way; 365&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) dedicated road right-of-way for the use of public transit, such as bus rapid transit; 366&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;or 367&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) fixed-route bus corridors subject to an interlocal agreement or contract between a 368&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;municipality or county and: 369&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) a public transit district as defined in Section 17B-2a-802 ; or 370&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) an eligible political subdivision as defined in Section 59-12-2219 . 371&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(40) "Moderate income housing" means housing occupied or reserved for occupancy 372&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;by households with a gross household income equal to or less than 80% of the median gross 373&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;income for households of the same size in the county in which the city is located. 374&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(41) "Municipal utility easement" means an easement that: 375&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) is created or depicted on a plat recorded in a county recorder's office and is 376&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;described as a municipal utility easement granted for public use; 377&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) is not a protected utility easement or a public utility easement as defined in Section 378&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; 54-3-27 ; 379&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) the municipality or the municipality's affiliated governmental entity uses and 380&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;occupies to provide a utility service, including sanitary sewer, culinary water, electrical, storm 381&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;water, or communications or data lines; 382&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(d) is used or occupied with the consent of the municipality in accordance with an 383&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;authorized franchise or other agreement; 384&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(e) (i) is used or occupied by a specified public utility in accordance with an authorized 385&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;franchise or other agreement; and 386&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) is located in a utility easement granted for public use; or 387&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(f) is described in Section 10-9a-529 and is used by a specified public utility. 388&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(42) "Nominal fee" means a fee that reasonably reimburses a municipality only for time 389&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;spent and expenses incurred in: 390&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) verifying that building plans are identical plans; and 391&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) reviewing and approving those minor aspects of identical plans that differ from the 392&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;previously reviewed and approved building plans. 393&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(43) "Noncomplying structure" means a structure that: 394&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) legally existed before the structure's current land use designation; and 395&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) because of one or more subsequent land use ordinance changes, does not conform 396&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to the setback, height restrictions, or other regulations, excluding those regulations, which 397&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govern the use of land. 398&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(44) "Nonconforming use" means a use of land that: 399&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) legally existed before its current land use designation; 400&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) has been maintained continuously since the time the land use ordinance governing 401&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the land changed; and 402&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) because of one or more subsequent land use ordinance changes, does not conform 403&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to the regulations that now govern the use of the land. 404&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(45) "Official map" means a map drawn by municipal authorities and recorded in a 405&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;county recorder's office that: 406&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) shows actual and proposed rights-of-way, centerline alignments, and setbacks for 407&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;highways and other transportation facilities; 408&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) provides a basis for restricting development in designated rights-of-way or between 409&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;designated setbacks to allow the government authorities time to purchase or otherwise reserve 410&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the land; and 411&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) has been adopted as an element of the municipality's general plan. 412&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(46) "Parcel" means any real property that is not a lot. 413&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(47) (a) "Parcel boundary adjustment" means a recorded agreement between owners of 414&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adjoining parcels adjusting the mutual boundary, either by deed or by a boundary line 415&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;agreement in accordance with Section 10-9a-524 , if no additional parcel is created and: 416&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) none of the property identified in the agreement is a lot; or 417&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) the adjustment is to the boundaries of a single person's parcels. 418&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) "Parcel boundary adjustment" does not mean an adjustment of a parcel boundary 419&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;line that: 420&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) creates an additional parcel; or 421&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) constitutes a subdivision. 422&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) "Parcel boundary adjustment" does not include a boundary line adjustment made by 423&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the Department of Transportation. 424&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(48) "Person" means an individual, corporation, partnership, organization, association, 425&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;trust, governmental agency, or any other legal entity. 426&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(49) "Plan for moderate income housing" means a written document adopted by a 427&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;municipality's legislative body that includes: 428&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) an estimate of the existing supply of moderate income housing located within the 429&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;municipality; 430&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) an estimate of the need for moderate income housing in the municipality for the 431&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;next five years; 432&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) a survey of total residential land use; 433&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(d) an evaluation of how existing land uses and zones affect opportunities for moderate 434&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;income housing; and 435&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(e) a description of the municipality's program to encourage an adequate supply of 436&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;moderate income housing. 437&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(50) "Plat" means an instrument subdividing property into lots as depicted on a map or 438&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;other graphical representation of lands that a licensed professional land surveyor makes and 439&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prepares in accordance with Section 10-9a-603 or 57-8-13 . 440&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(51) "Potential geologic hazard area" means an area that: 441&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) is designated by a Utah Geological Survey map, county geologist map, or other 442&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;relevant map or report as needing further study to determine the area's potential for geologic 443&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hazard; or 444&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) has not been studied by the Utah Geological Survey or a county geologist but 445&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;presents the potential of geologic hazard because the area has characteristics similar to those of 446&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;a designated geologic hazard area. 447&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(52) "Public agency" means: 448&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) the federal government; 449&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) the state; 450&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) a county, municipality, school district, local district, special service district, or other 451&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;political subdivision of the state; or 452&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(d) a charter school. 453&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(53) "Public hearing" means a hearing at which members of the public are provided a 454&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;reasonable opportunity to comment on the subject of the hearing. 455&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(54) "Public meeting" means a meeting that is required to be open to the public under 456&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Title 52, Chapter 4, Open and Public Meetings Act. 457&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(55) "Public street" means a public right-of-way, including a public highway, public 458&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avenue, public boulevard, public parkway, public road, public lane, public alley, public 459&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viaduct, public subway, public tunnel, public bridge, public byway, other public transportation 460&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;easement, or other public way. 461&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(56) "Receiving zone" means an area of a municipality that the municipality 462&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;designates, by ordinance, as an area in which an owner of land may receive a transferable 463&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;development right. 464&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(57) "Record of survey map" means a map of a survey of land prepared in accordance 465&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with Section 10-9a-603 , 17-23-17 , 17-27a-603 , or 57-8-13 . 466&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(58) "Residential facility for persons with a disability" means a residence: 467&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) in which more than one person with a disability resides; and 468&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) (i) which is licensed or certified by the Department of Human Services under Title 469&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;62A, Chapter 2, Licensure of Programs and Facilities; or 470&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) which is licensed or certified by the Department of Health under Title 26, Chapter 471&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;21, Health Care Facility Licensing and Inspection Act. 472&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(59) "Rules of order and procedure" means a set of rules that govern and prescribe in a 473&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;public meeting: 474&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) parliamentary order and procedure; Station area plan requirements -- Contents -- Review and 822&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; certification by applicable metropolitan planning organization. 823&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (1) As used in this section: 824&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (a) "Applicable metropolitan planning organization" means the metropolitan planning 825&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; organization that has jurisdiction over the area in which a fixed guideway public transit station 826&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; is located. 827&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (b) "Applicable public transit district" means the public transit district, as defined in 828&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Section 17B-2a-802 , of which a fixed guideway public transit station is included. 829&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (c) "Existing fixed guideway public transit station" means a fixed guideway public 830&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; transit station for which construction begins before June 1, 2022. 831&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (d) "Fixed guideway" means the same as that term is defined in Section 59-12-102 . 832&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (e) "Metropolitan planning organization" means an organization established under 23 833&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; U.S.C. Sec. 134. 834&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (f) "New fixed guideway public transit station" means a fixed guideway public transit 835&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; station for which construction begins on or after June 1, 2022. 836&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (g) "Qualifying land use application" means a land use application: 837&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) that involves land located within a station area for an existing public transit station 838&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; that provides rail services; 839&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) that involves land located within a station area for which the municipality has not 840&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; yet satisfied the requirements of Subsection (2)(a); 841&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (iii) that proposes the development of an area greater than five contiguous acres; 842&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (iv) that would require the municipality to amend the municipality's general plan or 843&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; change a zoning designation for the land use application to be approved; 844&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (v) that would require a higher density than the density currently allowed by the 845&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; municipality; 846&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (vi) that proposes the construction of new residential units, at least 10% of which are 847&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; dedicated to moderate income housing; and 848&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (vii) for which the land use applicant requests the municipality to initiate the process of 849&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; satisfying the requirements of Subsection (2)(a) for the station area in which the development 850&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; is proposed, subject to Subsection (3)(d). 851&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (h) (i) "Station area" means: 852&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (A) for a fixed guideway public transit station that provides rail services, the area 853&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; within a one-half mile radius of the center of the fixed guideway public transit station platform; 854&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; or 855&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (B) for a fixed guideway public transit station that provides bus services only, the area 856&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; within a one-fourth mile radius of the center of the fixed guideway public transit station 857&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; platform. 858&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) "Station area" includes any parcel bisected by the radius limitation described in 859&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Subsection (1)(h)(i)(A) or (B). 860&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) "Station area plan" means a plan that: 861&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) establishes a vision, and the actions needed to implement that vision, for the 862&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; development of land within a station area; and 863&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) is developed and adopted in accordance with this section. 864&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (2) (a) Subject to the requirements of this section, a municipality that has a fixed 865&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; guideway public transit station located within the municipality's boundaries shall, for the 866&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; station area: 867&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) develop and adopt a station area plan; and 868&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) adopt any appropriate land use regulations to implement the station area plan. 869&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (b) The requirements of Subsection (2)(a) shall be considered satisfied if: 870&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) (A) the municipality has already taken actions to satisfy the requirements of 871&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Subsection (2)(a) for a station area, including actions that involve public and stakeholder 872&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; engagement processes, market assessments, the creation of a station area vision, planning and 873&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) (A) the municipality has determined that conditions exist that make satisfying a 878&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; portion or all of the requirements of Subsection (2)(a) for a station area impracticable, 879&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; including conditions that relate to existing development, entitlements, land ownership, land 880&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; uses that make opportunities for new development and long-term redevelopment infeasible, 881&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; or 964&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (D) any other similar action that promotes the objective described in Subsection 965&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (7)(a)(i). 966&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) To promote the objective described in Subsection (7)(a)(ii), a municipality may 967&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; consider implementing the following actions: 968&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (A) conserving water resources through efficient land use; 969&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (B) improving air quality by reducing fuel consumption and motor vehicle trips; 970&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (C) establishing parks, open spaces, and recreational opportunities; or 971&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (D) any other similar action that promotes the objective described in Subsection 972&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (7)(a)(ii). 973&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (iii) To promote the objective described in Subsection (7)(a)(iii), a municipality may 974&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; consider the following actions: 975&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (A) maintaining and improving the connections between housing, transit, employment, 976&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; education, recreation, and commerce; 977&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (B) encouraging mixed-use development; 978&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (C) enabling employment and educational opportunities within the station area; 979&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (D) encouraging and promoting enhanced broadband connectivity; or 980&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (E) any other similar action that promotes the objective described in Subsection 981&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (7)(a)(iii). 982&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (iv) To promote the objective described in Subsection (7)(a)(iv), a municipality may 983&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; consider the following: 984&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (A) supporting investment in infrastructure for all modes of transportation; 985&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (B) increasing utilization of public transit; 986&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (C) encouraging safe streets through the designation of pedestrian walkways and 987&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; bicycle lanes; 988&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (D) encouraging manageable and reliable traffic conditions; 989&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (E) aligning the station area plan with the regional transportation plan of the applicable 990&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; metropolitan planning organization; or 991&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (F) any other similar action that promotes the objective described in Subsection 992&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (7)(a)(iv). 993&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (8) A station area plan shall include the following components: 994&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (a) a station area vision that: 995&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) is consistent with Subsection (7); and 996&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) describes the following: 997&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (A) opportunities for the development of land within the station area under existing 998&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; conditions; 999&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (B) constraints on the development of land within the station area under existing 1000&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; conditions; 1001&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (C) the municipality's objectives for the transportation system within the station area 1002&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; and the future transportation system that meets those objectives; 1003&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (D) the municipality's objectives for land uses within the station area and the future 1004&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; land uses that meet those objectives; 1005&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (E) the municipality's objectives for public and open spaces within the station area and 1006&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; the future public and open spaces that meet those objectives; and 1007&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (F) the municipality's objectives for the development of land within the station area and 1008&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; the future development standards that meet those objectives; 1009&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (b) a map that depicts: 1010&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) the area within the municipality that is subject to the station area plan, provided that 1011&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; the station area plan may apply to areas outside of the station area; and 1012&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) the area where each action is needed to implement the station area plan; 1013&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (c) an implementation plan that identifies and describes each action needed within the 1014&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; next five years to implement the station area plan, and the party responsible for taking each 1015&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; action, including any actions to: 1016&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) modify land use regulations; 1017&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) make infrastructure improvements; 1018&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (iii) modify deeds or other relevant legal documents; 1019&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (iv) secure funding or develop funding strategies; 1020&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (v) establish design standards for development within the station area; or 1021&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (vi) provide environmental remediation; 1022&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (d) a statement that explains how the station area plan promotes the objectives 1023&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; described in Subsection (7)(a); and 1024&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (e) as an alternative or supplement to the requirements of Subsection (7) or (8), and for 1025&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; purposes of Subsection (2)(b)(ii), a statement that describes any conditions that would make 1026&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; the following impracticable: 1027&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (i) promoting the objectives described in Subsection (7)(a); or 1028&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (ii) satisfying the requirements of Subsection (8). 1029&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (9) A municipality shall develop a station area plan with the involvement of all 1030&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; relevant stakeholders that have an interest in the station area through public outreach and 1031&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; community engagement, including: 1032&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (a) other impacted communities; 1033&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (b) the applicable public transit district; 1034&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (c) the applicable metropolitan planning organization; 1035&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (d) the Department of Transportation; 1036&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (e) owners of property within the station area; and 1037&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (f) the municipality's residents and business owners. 1038&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (10) (a) A municipality that is required to develop and adopt a station area plan for a 1039&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; station area under this section shall submit to the applicable metropolitan planning organization 1040&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; and the applicable public transit district documentation evidencing that the municipality has 1041&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; satisfied the requirement of Subsection (2)(a)(i) for the station area, including: 1042&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; or 1455&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) will suffer a damage different in kind than, or an injury distinct from, that of the 1456&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;general community as a result of the land use decision. 1457&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(3) "Affected entity" means a county, municipality, local district, special service 1458&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;district under Title 17D, Chapter 1, Special Service District Act, school district, interlocal 1459&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;cooperation entity established under Title 11, Chapter 13, Interlocal Cooperation Act, specified 1460&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;property owner, property owner's association, public utility, or the Utah Department of 1461&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Transportation, if: 1462&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) the entity's services or facilities are likely to require expansion or significant 1463&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;modification because of an intended use of land; 1464&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) the entity has filed with the county a copy of the entity's general or long-range plan; 1465&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;or 1466&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) the entity has filed with the county a request for notice during the same calendar 1467&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;year and before the county provides notice to an affected entity in compliance with a 1468&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;requirement imposed under this chapter. 1469&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(4) "Affected owner" means the owner of real property that is: 1470&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) a single project; 1471&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) the subject of a land use approval that sponsors of a referendum timely challenged 1472&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in accordance with Subsection 20A-7-601 [ (5) ] (6) ; and 1473&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) determined to be legally referable under Section 20A-7-602.8 . 1474&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(5) "Appeal authority" means the person, board, commission, agency, or other body 1475&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;designated by ordinance to decide an appeal of a decision of a land use application or a 1476&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;variance. 1477&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(6) "Billboard" means a freestanding ground sign located on industrial, commercial, or 1478&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;residential property if the sign is designed or intended to direct attention to a business, product, 1479&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;or service that is not sold, offered, or existing on the property where the sign is located. 1480&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(7) (a) "Charter school" means: 1481&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) an operating charter school; 1482&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) a charter school applicant that a charter school authorizer approves in accordance 1483&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with Title 53G, Chapter 5, Part 3, Charter School Authorization; or 1484&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) an entity that is working on behalf of a charter school or approved charter 1485&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;applicant to develop or construct a charter school building. 1486&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) "Charter school" does not include a therapeutic school. 1487&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(8) "Chief executive officer" means the person or body that exercises the executive 1488&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;powers of the county. 1489&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(9) "Conditional use" means a land use that, because of the unique characteristics or 1490&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;potential impact of the land use on the county, surrounding neighbors, or adjacent land uses, 1491&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;may not be compatible in some areas or may be compatible only if certain conditions are 1492&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;required that mitigate or eliminate the detrimental impacts. 1493&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(10) "Constitutional taking" means a governmental action that results in a taking of 1494&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;private property so that compensation to the owner of the property is required by the: 1495&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) Fifth or Fourteenth Amendment of the Constitution of the United States; or 1496&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) Utah Constitution, Article I, Section 22. 1497&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(11) "County utility easement" means an easement that: 1498&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) a plat recorded in a county recorder's office described as a county utility easement 1499&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;or otherwise as a utility easement; 1502&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) the county or the county's affiliated governmental entity owns or creates; and 1503&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(d) (i) either: 1504&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) no person uses or occupies; or 1508&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) a person uses or occupies with or without an authorized franchise or other 1509&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;agreement with the county. 1510&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(12) "Culinary water authority" means the department, agency, or public entity with 1511&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;responsibility to review and approve the feasibility of the culinary water system and sources for 1512&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the subject property. 1513&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(13) "Development activity" means: 1514&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) any construction or expansion of a building, structure, or use that creates additional 1515&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;demand and need for public facilities; or 1518&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) any change in the use of land that creates additional demand and need for public 1519&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;facilities. 1520&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(14) (a) "Development agreement" means a written agreement or amendment to a 1521&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;written agreement between a county and one or more parties that regulates or controls the use 1522&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;or development of a specific area of land. 1523&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) "Development agreement" does not include an improvement completion assurance. 1524&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(15) (a) "Disability" means a physical or mental impairment that substantially limits 1525&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;one or more of a person's major life activities, including a person having a record of such an 1526&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;impairment or being regarded as having such an impairment. 1527&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) "Disability" does not include current illegal use of, or addiction to, any federally 1528&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;controlled substance, as defined in Section 102 of the Controlled Substances Act, 21 U.S.C. 1529&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Sec. 802. 1530&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(16) "Educational facility": 1531&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) means: 1532&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) a school district's building at which pupils assemble to receive instruction in a 1533&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;program for any combination of grades from preschool through grade 12, including 1534&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kindergarten and a program for children with disabilities; 1535&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) a structure or facility: 1536&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) located on the same property as a building described in Subsection (16)(a)(i); and 1537&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) used in support of the use of that building; and 1538&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) a building to provide office and related space to a school district's administrative 1539&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;personnel; and 1540&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) does not include: 1541&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) land or a structure, including land or a structure for inventory storage, equipment 1542&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;storage, food processing or preparing, vehicle storage or maintenance, or similar use that is: 1543&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) not located on the same property as a building described in Subsection (16)(a)(i); 1544&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and 1545&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) used in support of the purposes of a building described in Subsection (16)(a)(i); or 1546&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) a therapeutic school. 1547&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(17) "Fire authority" means the department, agency, or public entity with responsibility 1548&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to review and approve the feasibility of fire protection and suppression services for the subject 1549&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;property. 1550&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(18) "Flood plain" means land that: 1551&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) is within the 100-year flood plain designated by the Federal Emergency 1552&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Management Agency; and 1702&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) reviewing and approving those minor aspects of identical plans that differ from the 1703&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;previously reviewed and approved building plans. 1704&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(46) "Noncomplying structure" means a structure that: 1705&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) legally existed before the structure's current land use designation; and 1706&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) because of one or more subsequent land use ordinance changes, does not conform 1707&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to the setback, height restrictions, or other regulations, excluding those regulations that govern 1708&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the use of land. 1709&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(47) "Nonconforming use" means a use of land that: 1710&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) legally existed before the current land use designation; 1711&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) has been maintained continuously since the time the land use ordinance regulation 1712&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;governing the land changed; and 1713&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) because of one or more subsequent land use ordinance changes, does not conform 1714&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;to the regulations that now govern the use of the land. 1715&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(48) "Official map" means a map drawn by county authorities and recorded in the 1716&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;county recorder's office that: 1717&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) shows actual and proposed rights-of-way, centerline alignments, and setbacks for 1718&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;highways and other transportation facilities; or 1901&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) if a county has designated a mountainous planning district, for growth and 1902&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;development of all or any part of the land within the mountainous planning district;
