2415&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(1) (a) (i) An applicant who has submitted a complete land use application, including 2416&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the payment of all application fees, is entitled to substantive review of the application under the 2417&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;land use regulations: 2418&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) in effect on the date that the application is complete; and 2419&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) applicable to the application or to the information shown on the submitted 2420&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;application. 2421&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) An applicant is entitled to approval of a land use application if the application 2422&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;conforms to the requirements of the applicable land use regulations, land use decisions, and 2423&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;development standards in effect when the applicant submits a complete application and pays all 2424&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;application fees, unless: 2425&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) the land use authority, on the record, formally finds that a compelling, 2426&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;countervailing public interest would be jeopardized by approving the application and specifies 2427&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the compelling, countervailing public interest in writing; or 2428&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) in the manner provided by local ordinance and before the applicant submits the 2429&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;application, the county formally initiates proceedings to amend the county's land use 2430&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;regulations in a manner that would prohibit approval of the application as submitted. 2431&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) The county shall process an application without regard to proceedings the county 2432&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;initiated to amend the county's ordinances as described in Subsection (1)(a)(ii)(B) if: 2433&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) 180 days have passed since the county initiated the proceedings; and 2434&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) the proceedings have not resulted in an enactment that prohibits approval of the 2435&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;application as submitted. 2436&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(c) A land use application is considered submitted and complete when the applicant 2437&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;provides the application in a form that complies with the requirements of applicable ordinances 2438&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and pays all applicable fees. 2439&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(d) The continuing validity of an approval of a land use application is conditioned upon 2440&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the applicant proceeding after approval to implement the approval with reasonable diligence. 2441&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(e) A county may not impose on an applicant who has submitted a complete 2442&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;application a requirement that is not expressed: 2443&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) in this chapter; 2444&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) in a county ordinance; or 2445&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) in a county specification for public improvements applicable to a subdivision or 2446&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;development that is in effect on the date that the applicant submits an application. 2447&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(f) A county may not impose on a holder of an issued land use permit or a final, 2448&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;unexpired subdivision plat a requirement that is not expressed: 2449&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) in a land use permit; 2450&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) on the subdivision plat; 2451&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) in a document on which the land use permit or subdivision plat is based; 2452&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iv) in the written record evidencing approval of the land use permit or subdivision 2453&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;plat; 2454&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(v) in this chapter; or 2455&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(vi) in a county ordinance. 2456&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(g) Except as provided in Subsection (1)(h), a county may not withhold issuance of a 2457&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;certificate of occupancy or acceptance of subdivision improvements because of an applicant's 2458&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;failure to comply with a requirement that is not expressed: 2459&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) in the building permit or subdivision plat, documents on which the building permit 2460&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;or subdivision plat is based, or the written record evidencing approval of the building permit or 2461&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;subdivision plat; or 2462&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) in this chapter or the county's ordinances. 2463&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(h) A county may not unreasonably withhold issuance of a certificate of occupancy 2464&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;where an applicant has met all requirements essential for the public health, public safety, and 2465&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;general welfare of the occupants, in accordance with this chapter, unless: 2466&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(i) the applicant and the county have agreed in a written document to the withholding 2467&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of a certificate of occupancy; or 2468&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) the applicant has not provided a financial assurance for required and uncompleted 2469&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;landscaping or infrastructure improvements in accordance with an applicable ordinance that the 2470&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;legislative body adopts under this chapter. 2471&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(2) A county is bound by the terms and standards of applicable land use regulations and 2472&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;shall comply with mandatory provisions of those regulations. 2473&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(3) A county may not, as a condition of land use application approval, require a person 2474&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;filing a land use application to obtain documentation regarding a school district's willingness, 2475&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;capacity, or ability to serve the development proposed in the land use application. 2476&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(4) (a) Except as provided in Subsection (4)(b), for a period of 10 years after the day on 2477&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;which a subdivision plat is recorded, a county may not impose on a building permit applicant 2478&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;for a single-family dwelling located within the subdivision any land use regulation that is 2479&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;enacted within 10 years after the day on which the subdivision plat is recorded. 2480&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) Subsection (4)(a) does not apply to any changes in the requirements of the 2481&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;applicable building code, health code, or fire code, or other similar regulations. 2482&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(5) Upon a specified public agency's submission of a development plan and schedule as 2483&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;required in Subsection 17-27a-305 (8) that complies with the requirements of that subsection, 2484&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the specified public the 2486&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;date of submission. 2487&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(6) (a) If sponsors of a referendum timely challenge a project in accordance with 2488&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Subsection 20A-7-601 [ (5) ] (6) , the project's affected owner may rescind the project's land use 2489&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;approval by delivering a 3591&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) paved pedestrian or paved nonmotorized transportation projects that: 3592&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) mitigate traffic congestion on the state highway system; and 3593&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) are part of an active transportation plan approved by the department; 3594&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iii) public transit projects that directly add capacity to the public transit systems within 3595&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the state, not including facilities ancillary to the public transit system; and 3596&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(iv) pedestrian or nonmotorized transportation projects that provide connection to a 3597&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;public transit system. 3598&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(b) (i) A local government or district may nominate a project for prioritization in 3599&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;accordance with the process established by the commission in rule. 3600&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(ii) If a local government or district nominates a project for prioritization by the 3601&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;commission, the local government or district shall provide data and evidence to show that: 3602&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(A) the project will advance the purposes and goals described in Section 72-1-211 ; 3603&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(B) for a public transit project, the local government or district has an ongoing funding 3604&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;source for operations and maintenance of the proposed development; and 3605&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(C) the local government or district will provide 40% of the costs for the project as 3606&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;required by Subsection 72-2-124 (4)(a)(viii) or 72-2-124 (9)(e). 3607&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(2) The following shall be included in the written prioritization process under 3608&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Subsection (1): 3609&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(a) a description of how the strategic initiatives of the department adopted under 3610&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Section 72-1-211 are advanced by the written prioritization process;
